.

Algorithm 7

Contamination-adaptive classification with marginal coverage

Below, Theorem
A2
establishes that the marginal coverage of the prediction sets output by Algorithm
7
is bounded from below by
1-\alpha
, as long as our method is applied based on the true model matrix
M
in (
10
) and the correct contaminated label frequencies
\tilde{\rho}_{k}
for all
k\in[K]
.

Theorem A2
.

Suppose
(X_{i},Y_{i},\tilde{Y}_{i})
are i.i.d. for all
i\in[n+1]
.
Assume the label contamination model in Assumption
1
holds.
Fix any prediction function
\mathcal{C}
satisfying Definition
1
, and let
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})
indicate the prediction set output by Algorithm
7
based on the inverse
V
of the model matrix
M
in the label contamination model (
10
) and the true values of the contaminated label frequencies
\tilde{\rho}_{k}
for all
k\in[K]
.
Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]\geq 1-\alpha.

Next, we prove that Algorithm
7
is not overly conservative, following an approach similar to that of Theorem
3
for Algorithm
1
.
This requires a technical lower bound for the marginal coverage inflation factor
\Delta(t)
, whose interpretation is similar to that of Assumption
4
.

Assumption 6
.

The factor
\Delta(t)
in (
A74
) is bounded from below by:

\displaystyle\inf_{t\in(0,1)}\Delta(t)\geq-\alpha+c(n_{\mathrm{cal}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\frac{1+2\sqrt{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}.

Under this setup, an upper bound for the marginal coverage of the conformal prediction sets output by Algorithm
7
is established below.

Theorem A3
.

Under the setup of Theorem
A2
, let
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})
indicate the prediction set output by Algorithm
7
based on the inverse
V
of the model matrix
M
in (
10
) and the true values of the label frequencies
\rho_{k},\tilde{\rho}_{k}
for all
k\in[K]
.
Suppose also that Assumptions
2
,
3
, and
6
hold, and that
\tilde{\rho}_{k}>0
for all
k\in[K]
. Then,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]\leq 1-\alpha+\varphi^{\mathrm{marg}}_{k}(n_{k},n_{*}),

where

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varphi^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})=2\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}\cdot\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}1/j}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}\cdot\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}+\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right].\end{split}

(A77)

The interpretation of Theorem
A3
is analogous to that of Theorem
3
: the marginal coverage of the prediction sets output by Algorithm
7
is guaranteed to be asymptotically tight because
\varphi^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{k},n_{*})\to 0
as
n_{*}\to\infty
.

We conclude this section by noting that the power of Algorithm
7
can be further boosted without losing marginal coverage, as long as a relatively mild “optimistic” assumption on the marginal coverage inflation factor defined in (
A68
) holds.
Concretely, we propose to apply Algorithm
7
with the set
\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}
in (
A73
) replaced by:

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}:=\left\{i\in[n_{\mathrm{cal}}]:\frac{i}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}\geq 1-\alpha-\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*}),-\frac{(1-\alpha)}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}\right\}\right\}.

(A78)

This optimistic variation of Algorithm
7
is analogous to the extension of Algorithm
1
presented earlier in Section
3.1.4
, and it enjoys a similar coverage guarantee.

Proposition A3
.

Under the same setup as Theorem
A2
, assume also that
\inf_{t\in{\rm I}\kern-1.47495pt{\rm R}}\Delta(t)\geq\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})-(1-\alpha)/n_{\mathrm{cal}}
.
If
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})
is the prediction set output by Algorithm
7
applied with
\hat{\mathcal{I}}
in (
A78
) instead of (
A73
), then
\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]\geq 1-\alpha
.

A6.2
Adaptive prediction sets with calibration-conditional coverage

The methods presented in this paper can also be extended to construct prediction sets
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})
guaranteeing the following notion of calibration-conditional coverage
[
24
]
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\geq 1-\alpha\right]\geq 1-\gamma,

(A79)

for any given
\alpha,\gamma\in(0,1)
.
Concretely, we present here Algorithm
8
, which extends for the aforementioned purpose Algorithm
1
from Section
1
.
Analogous extensions of the methods described in Sections
3.2
–
3.3
and Section
A6.1
would also follow similarly.
In a nutshell, Algorithm
8
differs from Algorithm
1
only in that it uses a finite-sample correction factor

\displaystyle\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)

\displaystyle:=\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma_{1})}{2n_{k}}}+2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\gamma_{2})}{2n_{*}}},

(A80)

where

\displaystyle\gamma_{1}=\gamma\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|}\right),

\displaystyle\gamma_{2}=\frac{\gamma}{2}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|},

instead of the factor
\delta(n_{k},n_{*})
defined in (
18
).
Note that the second term on the right-hand-side of (
A80
) simply vanishes in the special case where
\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|=0
, which corresponds to the absence of label contamination.

1

Input:

Data set
\{(X_{i},\tilde{Y}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
with corrupted labels
\tilde{Y}_{i}\in[K]
.

The inverse
V
of the matrix
M
in (
10
).

Unlabeled test point with features
X_{n+1}
.

Machine learning algorithm
\mathcal{A}
for training a
K
-class classifier.

Prediction function
\mathcal{C}
satisfying Definition
1
; e.g., (
2
).

Desired coverage level
1-\alpha\in(0,1)

Desired calibration-conditional significance level
\gamma\in(0,1)
.

Randomly split
[n]
into two disjoint subsets,
\mathcal{D}^{\text{train}}
and
\mathcal{D}^{\mathrm{cal}}
.

2

Train the classifier
\mathcal{A}
on the data in
\mathcal{D}^{\text{train}}
.

3

Compute conformity scores
\hat{s}(X_{i},k)
using (
1
) for all
i\in\mathcal{D}^{\mathrm{cal}}
and all
k\in[K]
.

4

Define the empirical CDF
\hat{F}_{l}^{k}
of
\{\hat{s}(X_{i},k):i\in\mathcal{D}_{l}^{\mathrm{cal}}\}
for all
l\in[K]
, as in (
13
).

5

for

k=1,\dots,K

do

6

Define
\mathcal{D}_{k}^{\mathrm{cal}}=\{i\in\mathcal{D}^{\mathrm{cal}}:\tilde{Y}_{i}=k\}
and
n_{k}=|\mathcal{D}_{k}^{\mathrm{cal}}|
.

7

Sort
\{\hat{s}(X_{i},k):i\in\mathcal{D}_{k}^{\mathrm{cal}}\}
into
(S^{k}_{(1)},S^{k}_{(2)},\dots,S^{k}_{(n_{k})})
, in ascending order.

8

Compute
\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})
for all
i\in[n_{k}]
and
l\in[K]
.

9

Compute
{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})
for all
i\in[n_{k}]
, as in (
14
).

10

Compute
\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)
using (
A80
).

11

Construct the set
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}\subseteq[K]
as in (
15
), using
\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)
instead of
\delta(n_{k},n_{*})
.

12

Evaluate
\hat{\tau}_{k}
based on
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
as in (
16
).

13

end for

14

Evaluate
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})=\mathcal{C}(X_{n+1},\hat{\tau};\hat{\pi})
, where
\hat{\tau}=(\hat{\tau}_{1},\ldots,\hat{\tau}_{K})
.

15

Output:
Conformal prediction set
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})
for
Y_{n+1}
.

Algorithm 8

Extension of Algorithm
1
with calibration-conditional coverage

Theorem A4
.

Suppose
(X_{i},Y_{i},\tilde{Y}_{i})
are i.i.d. for all
i\in[n+1]
.
Assume the label contamination model in Assumption
1
holds.
Fix any prediction function
\mathcal{C}
satisfying Definition
1
, and let
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})
indicate the prediction set output by Algorithm
8
based on the inverse
V
of the model matrix
M
in the label contamination model (
10
).
Then, for any
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\geq 1-\alpha\right]\geq 1-\gamma.

It is interesting to compare the finite-sample correction factor in (
A80
) to the standard approach for constructing conformal prediction sets with calibration-conditional coverage.
In fact, Proposition 2a in
[
24
, ]
implies that calibration-conditional coverage (
A79
) can be achieved in the absence of label contamination by simply applying Algorithm
3
, in Section
A1.2
, with the nominal level
\alpha
replaced by

\displaystyle\alpha^{\prime}=\alpha-\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}.

Intuitively, this correction is similar to the first term on the right-hand-side of (
A80
), and it becomes equivalent in the case that the matrix
V
is diagonal, which corresponds to the absence of label contamination.

Following an approach similar to that of Theorem
3
, it can be proved that Algorithm
8
is not overly conservative.
This requires a technical lower bound for the coverage inflation factor
\Delta_{k}(t)
, whose interpretation is similar to that of Assumption
4
.

Assumption 7
.

The coverage inflation factor
\Delta_{k}(t)
is bounded from below by:

\displaystyle\inf_{t\in(0,1)}\Delta_{k}(t)\geq-\alpha+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma_{1})}{2n_{k}}}+4\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\gamma_{2})}{2n_{*}}}.

Under this setup, a finite-sample upper bound for the marginal coverage of the conformal prediction sets output by Algorithm
8
is established below.

Theorem A5
.

Suppose
(X_{i},Y_{i},\tilde{Y}_{i})
are i.i.d. for all
i\in[n+1]
.
Assume the label contamination model in Assumption
1
holds.
Fix any prediction function
\mathcal{C}
satisfying Definition
1
, and let
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})
indicate the prediction set output by Algorithm
8
based on the inverse
V
of the model matrix
M
in the label contamination model (
10
).
Suppose also that Assumptions
2
,
3
, and
7
hold.
Then, for any
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\leq 1-\alpha+\varphi_{k}^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)\right]\geq 1-\gamma.

where

\displaystyle\varphi_{k}^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)

\displaystyle=\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+\frac{1}{n_{k}}\left(1+\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\gamma\bar{v}}\right)+\sqrt{\frac{\log[3/(\gamma\bar{v})]}{2n_{k}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log[3/(\gamma\bar{v})]}{2n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}}\cdot\left[\log(n_{k}+1)+\frac{3}{\gamma\bar{v}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right],

and

\displaystyle\bar{v}=\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|}\right).

The interpretation of Theorem
A5
is similar to that of Theorem
3
, because
\varphi_{k}^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)\to 0
as
n_{*}\to\infty
, for any fixed
\gamma>0
.

We conclude this section by noting that the power of Algorithm
8
can also be further boosted without losing the calibration-conditional coverage guarantee, as long as a relatively mild “optimistic” assumption on the coverage inflation factor defined in (
7
) holds.
Concretely, we propose to apply Algorithm
8
with the set
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
in (
15
) replaced by:

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}:=\left\{i\in[n_{k}]:\frac{i}{n_{k}}\geq 1-\alpha-\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma),-\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}\right\}\right\}.

(A81)

The motivation behind this approach is that, in the absence of label contamination, valid calibration-conditional coverage (
A79
) can be achieved by simply applying Algorithm
3
with the nominal level
\alpha
replaced by
\alpha^{\prime}=\alpha-\sqrt{\log(1/\gamma)/(2n_{k})}

[
24
]
.
Therefore, in analogy with the optimistic variation of Algorithm
8
presented earlier in Section
3.1.4
, it is now intuitive to propose an optimistic extension of Algorithm
8
that is never more conservative than the standard benchmark for clean data.
The following result establishes that this optimistic approach still guarantees the desired calibration-conditional coverage (
A79
), as long as some relatively mild assumption on the coverage inflation factor holds.

Proposition A4
.

Under the setup of Theorem
A4
, assume also that
\inf_{t\in{\rm I}\kern-1.47495pt{\rm R}}\Delta_{k}(t)\geq\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*})-\sqrt{\log(1/\gamma)/(2n_{k})}
.
If
\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})
is the set output by Algorithm
8
applied with
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
in (
A81
), then,
\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\geq 1-\alpha\right]\geq 1-\gamma
for any
k\in[K]
.

Appendix A7
Mathematical proofs

A7.1
Preliminaries

Proof of Theorem
1
.

By definition of the conformity score function in (
1
),
Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})
if and only if
\hat{s}(X_{n+1},Y_{n+1})\leq\hat{\tau}_{Y_{n+1}}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y_{n+1}=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y_{n+1}=k\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right],

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y_{n+1}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq\hat{\tau}_{k}\mid Y_{n+1}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq\hat{\tau}_{k}\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[F_{k}^{k}(\hat{\tau}_{k})-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(\hat{\tau}_{k})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{k}({\widehat{\tau}}_{k})\right].

Above, the notation
\mathbb{E}\left[\cdot\mid\mathcal{D}\right]
indicates the expected value conditional on the training and calibration data sets.
From this, Equations (
8
) and (
9
) follow directly because

\displaystyle 1-\alpha\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]\leq 1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{k}+1},

by Proposition
A1
, which applies here since the data pairs
(X_{i},\tilde{Y}_{i})
are i.i.d. random samples.
∎

Proof of Proposition
1
.

By definition of
\tilde{P}_{k}
and
P_{l}
,

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{k}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[X\mid\tilde{Y}=k\right]=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[X,Y=l\mid\tilde{Y}=k\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbb{P}\left[X,\tilde{Y}=k\mid Y=l\right]\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[Y=l\right]}{\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}=k\right]}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\frac{\mathbb{P}\left[X\mid Y=l\right]\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}=k\mid Y=l\right]\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[Y=l\right]}{\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}=k\right]}=\sum_{l=1}^{K}P_{l}\cdot M_{kl}.

Above, the fourth equality follows directly from Assumption
1
.
∎

Proof of Corollary
1
.

By Theorem
1
, it suffices to prove that
\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{k}(t)\right]\geq 0
for all
t\in\mathbb{R}
and
k\in[K]
.
To establish that, note that combining (
7
) with Proposition
1
gives:

\displaystyle\Delta_{k}(t)

\displaystyle=F_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)=(1-M_{kk})F_{k}^{k}(t)-\sum_{j\neq k}M_{kj}F_{j}^{k}(t)

\displaystyle\geq(1-M_{kk})F_{k}^{k}(t)-\bigg(\sum_{j\neq k}M_{kj}\bigg)\cdot\max_{j\neq k}F_{j}^{k}(t).

Further, by (
11
),

\displaystyle\Delta_{k}(t)

\displaystyle\geq(1-M_{kk})F_{k}^{k}(t)-\bigg(\sum_{j\neq k}M_{kj}\bigg)\cdot F_{k}^{k}(t)=\left(1-\sum_{j=1}^{K}M_{kj}\right)\cdot F_{k}^{k}(t)=0,

where the last equality follows from the fact that
\sum_{j=1}^{K}M_{kj}=1
for all
k\in[K]
.
This implies that
\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta_{k}({\widehat{\tau}}_{k})\right]\geq 0
, completing the proof.
∎

A7.2
Adaptive coverage under a known label contamination model

Proof of Theorem
2
.

Suppose
Y_{n+1}=k
, for some
k\in[K]
.
By definition of the conformity score function in (
1
), the event
k\notin\hat{C}(X_{n+1})
occurs if and only if
\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)>\hat{\tau}_{k}
.
We will assume without loss of generality that
\hat{\mathcal{I}_{k}}\neq\emptyset
and
\hat{i}_{k}=\min\{i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}_{k}}\}
; otherwise,
\hat{\tau}_{k}=1
and the result trivially holds.
As in the proof of Theorem
1
, the probability of miscoverage conditional on
Y_{n+1}=k
and on the labeled data in
\mathcal{D}
can be decomposed as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid\tilde{Y}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid\tilde{Y}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)>S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)>S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}\mid\tilde{Y}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\left[1-F_{k}^{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]-\left[1-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad=\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad=\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right]-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sup_{i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}}\left\{1-\frac{i}{n_{k}}-\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right\}-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}[\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)]+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}).

By definition of
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
, for all
i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
,

\displaystyle 1-\frac{i}{n_{k}}-\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\leq\alpha,

which implies a.s.

\displaystyle\sup_{i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}}\left\{1-\frac{i}{n_{k}}-\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right\}\leq\alpha.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}[\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)]\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}).

The second term on the right-hand-side above can be bound using the DKW inequality, as made precise by Lemma
A1
.
This leads to:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}).

In order to bound the last expected value, let
U_{1},\ldots,U_{n_{k}}
be i.i.d. uniform random variables on
[0,1]
, and denote their order statistics as
U_{(1)},\ldots,U_{(n_{k})}
. This implies that

\displaystyle\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\overset{d}{=}\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-U_{(\hat{i}_{k})}.

Therefore, it follows from (
17
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{i\in[n_{k}]}\left\{\frac{i}{n_{k}}-U_{(i)}\right\}\right]=c(n_{k}).

(A82)

We can thus conclude that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}+c(n_{k})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad=\alpha.

∎

Lemma A1
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
2
, for any
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|\right]\leq\frac{2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}.

Proof of Lemma
A1
.

Note that, for any
k\in[K]
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)

\displaystyle=(V_{kk}-1)\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left\{\left[\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)\right]-\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right)\max_{l\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|,

where in the second-to-last line we used the fact that
V
has row sums equal to 1 because it is the inverse of
M
, which has row sums equal to 1.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|\right]\leq 2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{l\in[K]}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right].

Define
l_{\min}:=\arg\min_{l\in[K]}n_{j}
and
n_{*}:=\min_{l\in[K]}n_{l}
.
Then, combining the DKW inequality with a union bound gives that, for any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{l\in[K]}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|>\eta\right]

\displaystyle\leq K\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{F}_{l_{\min}}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l_{\min}}(t)|>\eta\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2Ke^{-2n_{*}\eta^{2}}.

This implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{l\in[K]}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{l\in[K]}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|>\eta\right]d\eta

\displaystyle\leq 2K\int_{0}^{\infty}e^{-2n_{*}\eta^{2}}d\eta=K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2n_{*}}}.

Similarly, the DKW inequality also implies that, for any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|>2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]\leq\eta;

see Lemma
A2
.
Therefore, setting
\eta=1/n_{*}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2n_{*}}}+\frac{1}{n_{*}}\right].

∎

Lemma A2
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
2
, for any
k\in[K]
and
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|>2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\eta.

Proof of Lemma
A2
.

It follows from the definitions of
\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)
and
\Delta_{k}(t)
, and from the DKW inequality, that, for any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|>2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{j\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{j}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{j}(t)|>\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq K\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[|\hat{F}_{l_{\min}}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l_{\min}}(t)|>\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2K\cdot\exp\left[-2n_{*}\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=2K\cdot\exp\left[-\log(2K)-\log(1/\eta)\right]=\eta.

∎

Proof of Theorem
3
.

Define the events
\mathcal{A}_{1}=\{\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}=\emptyset\}
and
\mathcal{A}_{2}=\{\hat{i}_{k}=1\}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\right].\end{split}

(A83)

We will now separately bound the three terms on the right-hand-side of (
A83
).
The following notation will be useful for this purpose.
For all
i\in[n_{k}]
, let
U_{i}\sim\text{Uniform}(0,1)
be independent and identically distributed uniform random variables, and
denote their order statistics as
U_{(1)}<U_{(2)}<\ldots<U_{(n_{k})}
.

•

The probability of the event
\mathcal{A}_{1}
can be bound from above as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}=\emptyset\right]\leq\frac{1}{n_{*}}.

(A84)

To simplify the notation in the proof of (
A84
), define

\displaystyle d_{k}:=c(n_{k})+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right).

Then, under Assumption
4
,

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d_{k}+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\sup_{t\in[0,1]}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|.

Further, it follows from Lemma
A2
and (
18
) that, with probability at least
1-1/n_{*}
,

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d_{k}+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d_{k}+c(n_{k})+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right)=1.

This implies that the set
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
defined in (
15
) is non-empty with probability at least
1-1/n_{*}
, because
n_{k}\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
if
1-[\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})]\leq 1
.

•

Under
\mathcal{A}_{2}
, the second term on the right-hand-side of (
A83
) can be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq S^{k}_{(1)}\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]=F^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[F^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]\\
&\qquad=V_{kk}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]\\
&\qquad=V_{kk}\mathbb{E}\left[U_{(1)}\right]+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]\\
&\qquad=\frac{V_{kk}}{n_{k}+1}+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right].\end{split}

Next, using Assumption
3
, we can write:

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\right]&\leq\frac{V_{kk}}{n_{k}+1}+\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\\
&=\frac{V_{kk}}{n_{k}+1}+\mathbb{E}\left[U_{(1)}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right]\\
&=\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}+1}.\end{split}

(A85)

•

Under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
, by definition of
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
, for any
i\leq\hat{i}_{k}-1
,

\displaystyle\frac{i}{n_{k}}<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right).

Therefore, choosing
i=\hat{i}_{k}-1
, we get:

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right)+\frac{1}{n_{k}}.

As in the proof of Theorem
2
, the probability of coverage conditional on
Y_{n+1}=k
and on the labeled data in
\mathcal{D}
can be decomposed as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid\tilde{Y}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\mathbb{P}\left[Y\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid\tilde{Y}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}\mid\tilde{Y}=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+F_{k}^{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})=\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\quad=\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\quad<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})\right)+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\quad=1-\alpha+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)}).

To bound the last term above, note that

\displaystyle\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})

\displaystyle=(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)}))+(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)}))

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|+(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})),

where the expected value of the first term above can be bounded using Lemma
A1
, and the second term is given by

\displaystyle\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})

\displaystyle\qquad=(V_{kk}-1)\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right]+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\left(\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right)-\left(\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|.

Combining the above, we find that, under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|.

It remains to bound the expectation of the last term. By Assumption
2
,

\displaystyle\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|

\displaystyle\leq f_{\max}\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}|S^{k}_{(i)}-S^{k}_{(i-1)}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}|\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{=}}\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}(U_{(i)}-U_{(i-1)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i},

where
D_{1}=U_{(1)}
,
D_{i}=U_{(i)}-U_{(i-1)}
for
i=2,\dots,n_{k}
, and
D_{n_{k}+1}=1-U_{(n_{k})}
.
By a standard result on maximum uniform spacing,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i}\right]=\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}.

Therefore, under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|\right].

Thus, applying Lemma
A1
and the bound in (
A82
), it follows that, under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\right]\\
&\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{i\in[n_{k}]}\left(U_{i}-\frac{i}{n_{k}}\right)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\\
&\qquad=1-\alpha+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+c(n_{k})+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\\
&\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+c(n_{k})\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\\
&\qquad=1-\alpha+2\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}.\end{split}

(A86)

Finally, combining (
A83
) with (
A84
), (
A85
), and (
A86
) leads to the desired result:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}+1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+2\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}\left[1+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}+1}.

∎

Proof of Proposition
2
.

Suppose
Y_{n+1}=k
, for some
k\in[K]
. Proceeding exactly as in the proof of Theorem
2
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\max\{{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\{{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\}-(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}))-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad=\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-\max\{{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\{{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+(1-\alpha)/n_{k},0\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right)-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-\max\{{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|-\delta(n_{k},n_{*}),

using the fact that
\inf_{t\in{\rm I}\kern-1.47495pt{\rm R}}\Delta_{k}(t)\geq\delta(n_{k},n_{*})-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}
implies

\displaystyle|\max\{{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+(1-\alpha)/n_{k},0\}-\left(\Delta_{k}(t)-\delta(n_{k},n_{*})+(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right)|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|,

for all
t\in\mathbb{R}
.
The proof is then completed by proceeding as in the proof of Theorem
2
.
∎

A7.3
Adaptive coverage under a bounded label contamination model

Proof of Theorem
4
.

Suppose
Y_{n+1}=k
, for some
k\in[K]
.
By definition of the conformity score function in (
1
), the event
k\notin\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})
occurs if and only if
\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)>\hat{\tau}_{k}
.
We will assume without loss of generality that
\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}\neq\emptyset
and
\hat{i}_{k}=\min\{i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}\}
; otherwise,
\hat{\tau}_{k}=1
and the result trivially holds.
By proceeding exactly as in the proof of Theorem
2
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}[\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]+c(n_{k})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}).

The second term on the right-hand-side above can be bound using the DKW inequality, as made precise by Lemma
A3
.
This leads to:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right)}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\cdot\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+c(n_{k})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad=\alpha+2\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|-2\alpha_{V}\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha+2\left\{\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]-\alpha_{V}\right\}\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|.

Finally, taking an expectation with respect to the data used to estimate the confidence region for
V
cancels the last term on the right-hand-side above, leading to:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k\right]

\displaystyle\leq\alpha.

∎

Lemma A3
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
4
, for any
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}[\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t),0\right\}\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right)}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\cdot\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|.

Further,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|+(K-1)\left(\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right).

Proof of Lemma
A3
.

To simplify the notation in the following, define

\displaystyle\hat{\psi}_{k}:=\min_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right).

Note that
\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)
can be equivalently written as:

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)

\displaystyle:=\sum_{l\neq k}\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|.

Therefore, for any
k\in[K]
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)-\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left(\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)+\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)\left(\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)+\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)+\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)\left(\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)+\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}-\hat{\psi}_{k}\right)\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)+\hat{\psi}_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|.

Next, note that,

\displaystyle\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}-\hat{\psi}_{k}\right)\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}-\hat{\psi}_{k}\right)\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left[\max_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)-\hat{\psi}_{k}\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle=\hat{\zeta}_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|,

because
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\geq\hat{\psi}_{k}
for all
l\neq k
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\left|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right|\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\hat{\zeta}_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\hat{\psi}_{k}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|.

In the event that
V_{kl}\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}_{kl},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}]
for all
l\neq k
, which occurs with probability
1-\alpha_{V}
,

\displaystyle 0\leq\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\leq\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl},

\displaystyle 0\leq\hat{\psi}_{k}\leq\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl},

which implies
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}-\hat{\psi}_{k}\leq\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\leq\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}
.
Further, in this event,
\hat{\zeta}_{k}\leq\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}
.
Therefore, if
V_{kl}\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}_{kl},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}]
for all
l\neq k
,

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right).

Imagine now that it is not the case that
V_{kl}\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}_{kl},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}]
for all
l\neq k
.
Then,

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)-\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left(\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)+\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)\left(\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right|.

In conclusion,

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right|.

From here, proceeding as in the proof of Lemma
A1
, one easily arrives at:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t),0\right\}\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right)}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right)}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\cdot\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|.

This completes the first part of the proof.

To prove the second part, recall that

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)+\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)\left(\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\left|\sum_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right)\right|-|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)+\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right|+(K-1)\left(\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right|+(K-1)\left(\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right).

Then, in the event that
V_{kl}\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}_{kl},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}]
for all
l\neq k
,

\displaystyle\left|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right).

By contrast, if it is not the case that
V_{kl}\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}_{kl},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}]
for all
l\neq k
,

\displaystyle\left|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right|+(K-1)\left(\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+|V_{kl}|\right)+(K-1)\left(\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|

\displaystyle\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|+(K-1)\left(\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right).

From here, proceeding as in the proof of Lemma
A1
, one easily arrives at:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot 2\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right).

This completes the second part of the proof.
∎

Proof of Theorem
5
.

We follow the same strategy as in the proof of Theorem
3
.
Define the events
\mathcal{A}_{1}=\{\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}=\emptyset\}
and
\mathcal{A}_{2}=\{\hat{i}_{k}=1\}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right].\end{split}

(A87)

We will now separately bound the three terms on the right-hand-side of (
A87
).
The following notation will be useful for this purpose.
For all
i\in[n_{k}]
, let
U_{i}\sim\text{Uniform}(0,1)
be independent and identically distributed uniform random variables, and
denote their order statistics as
U_{(1)}<U_{(2)}<\ldots<U_{(n_{k})}
.

•

The probability of
\mathcal{A}_{1}
conditional on
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}
and
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}
can be bound from above as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}=\emptyset\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\leq\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right].

(A88)

To simplify the notation in the proof of (
A88
), define

\displaystyle d_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}

\displaystyle:=c(n_{k})+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right)}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+2\alpha_{V}\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right).

Then, under Assumption
5
,

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})+\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(i)})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}+\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\sup_{t\in[0,1]}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)|.

Further, it follows from the definition of
\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})
in (
22
) and Lemma
A4
applied with
\eta=1/n_{*}
that, with probability at least

1-\frac{1}{n_{*}}-\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right],

we have

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq 1-d_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}+\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)+2\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq 1-d_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}+c(n_{k})+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right)}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+2\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+2\alpha_{V}\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)=1.

This implies that the set
\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}
defined in (
15
), with
\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)
and
\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})
instead of
\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)
and
\delta(n_{k},n_{*})
, is non-empty with probability at least
1-1/n_{*}-\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]
, because
n_{k}\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}
if
1-[\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})]\leq 1
.

Under
\mathcal{A}_{2}
, the second term on the right-hand-side of (
A83
) can be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq S^{k}_{(1)}\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]=F^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)}).

Therefore, by the independence of
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}
and
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}
from the other data,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[F^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]\\
&\qquad=\mathbb{E}\left[U_{(1)}\right]+(V_{kk}-1)\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]\\
&\qquad=V_{kk}\mathbb{E}\left[U_{(1)}\right]+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right]\\
&\qquad=\frac{V_{kk}}{n_{k}+1}+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(1)})\right].\end{split}

Next, using Assumption
3
as in the proof of Theorem
3
, we arrive to:

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}+1}=\frac{1+\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|V_{kl}|-V_{kl}\right)}{n_{k}+1}\\
&\qquad\leq\frac{1+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}+1}.\end{split}
