Under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
, by definition of
\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}
, for any
i\leq\hat{i}_{k}-1
,

\displaystyle\frac{i}{n_{k}}<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right).

Therefore, choosing
i=\hat{i}_{k}-1
, we get:

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right)+\frac{1}{n_{k}}.

As in the proof of Theorem
3
, the probability of coverage conditional on
Y_{n+1}=k
, on
\mathcal{D}
, and on the confidence interval
[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]
can be decomposed as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right)+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)}).

To bound the last term above, note that

\displaystyle\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})

\displaystyle=(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)}))+(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)}))

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)|+(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})),

where the expected value of the first term above can be bounded using Lemma
A3
, and the second term is given by

\displaystyle\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})

\displaystyle\qquad=(V_{kk}-1)\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right]+\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\left(\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right)-\left(\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k}-1)})\right)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|.

Combining the above, we find that, under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|.

It remains to bound the expectation of the last term. By Assumption
2
,

\displaystyle\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|

\displaystyle\leq f_{\max}\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}|S^{k}_{(i)}-S^{k}_{(i-1)}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}|\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{=}}\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}(U_{(i)}-U_{(i-1)})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i},

where
D_{1}=U_{(1)}
,
D_{i}=U_{(i)}-U_{(i-1)}
for
i=2,\dots,n_{k}
, and
D_{n_{k}+1}=1-U_{(n_{k})}
.
By a standard result on maximum uniform spacing,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i}\right]=\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}.

Therefore, under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\\
&\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+c(n_{k})+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)|\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\\
&\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+c(n_{k})\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{2\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+|V_{kl}|\right)+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\\
&\qquad=1-\alpha+2\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]\cdot\sum_{l\neq k}\left(|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+|V_{kl}|\right)-2\alpha_{V}\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|\\
&\qquad\qquad+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\\
&\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+2\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}\\
&\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|\cdot\left(\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]-\alpha_{V}\right)\\
&\qquad\qquad+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}.\end{split}

(A90)

Above, the second inequality is obtained from Lemma
A3
and the bound in (
A82
).

Combining (
A87
) with (
A88
), (
A89
), and (
A90
) leads to:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]+\frac{1+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}+1}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+2\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|\cdot\left(\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]-\alpha_{V}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+2\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|\cdot\left(\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]-\alpha_{V}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{2}{n_{k}}\left[1+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right].

Finally, taking an expectation with respect to
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}}
and
\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}
, we arrive at:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{ci}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\alpha_{V}+2\mathbb{E}\left[\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right]+(K-1)\left(2\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{2}{n_{k}}\left[1+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\alpha_{V}+2c(n_{k})+4\alpha_{V}\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\sum_{l\neq k}\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right]}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(K-1)\left(2\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right]\right)+\frac{2}{n_{k}}\left[1+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\left(1+4\sum_{l\neq k}|\bar{V}_{kl}^{\mathrm{upp}}|\right)\alpha_{V}+2c(n_{k})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{4\sum_{l\neq k}\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{kl}\right]}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\min\left\{K\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+(K-1)\left(2\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right]\right)+\frac{2}{n_{k}}\left[1+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right].

∎

Lemma A4
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
4
, for any
k\in[K]
and
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|>(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)+2\Upsilon(K,\eta,n_{*})\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\eta\mathbb{I}\left[V\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right],

where
\Upsilon(K,\eta,n_{*}):=\sqrt{[\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)]/(2n_{*})}
.

Proof of Lemma
A4
.

By proceeding as in the proof of Lemma
A3
, we obtain that, in the event that
V\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]
, for any
k\in[K]
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{l\in[K]}\left|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)\right|\right)\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|+(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right).

Combined with the DKW inequality, this implies that, for any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}^{\mathrm{ci}}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|>(K-1)\left(2\hat{\delta}^{(V)}_{k*}+|\hat{\zeta}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{k}|\right)+2\Upsilon(K,\eta,n_{*})\sum_{l\neq k}|\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}|\mid\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{j\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{j}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{j}(t)|>\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[V\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\eta\mathbb{I}\left[V\in[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right]+\mathbb{I}\left[V\notin[\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}]\right].

Note that the last inequality above follows exactly as in the proof of Lemma
A2
.
∎

Proof of Proposition
3
.

This proof combines elements of the proofs of Proposition
2
, Theorem
2
, and Theorem
4
.
Suppose
Y_{n+1}=k
, for some
k\in[K]
. Proceeding exactly as in the proof of Theorem
2
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{low}},\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}-(\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}))

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\max\left\{\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}),\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\max\left\{\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}),\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})}),0\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-\max\left\{{\widehat{\Delta}}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}^{\mathrm{ci}}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t),0\right\},

using the assumption that
\inf_{t\in{\rm I}\kern-1.47495pt{\rm R}}\Delta_{k}(t)\geq\delta^{\mathrm{ci}}(n_{k},n_{*})-(1-\alpha)/n_{k}
in the third equality above.
The proof is then completed by proceeding as in the proof of Theorem
4
, using Lemma
A3
.

∎

A7.4
Fitting the label contamination model

Proof of Equation (
28
).

By definition of
Q
and
\tilde{Q}
in (
27
), for any
k,l\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{lk}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k\mid\tilde{Y}=l,\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=l^{\prime}\mid\tilde{Y}=l,\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[Y=l^{\prime}\mid\tilde{Y}=l,\hat{f}\right]\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k\mid Y=l^{\prime},\tilde{Y}=l,\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{K}M_{ll^{\prime}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k\mid Y=l^{\prime},\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{K}M_{ll^{\prime}}Q_{l^{\prime}k}

\displaystyle=(MQ)_{lk}.

∎

Proof of Equation (
A46
).

The proof of (
A46
) follows quite directly from Equation (
28
) and the definitions of
\tilde{\psi}
and
\psi
in Equation (
A47
):

\displaystyle\tilde{\psi}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,\tilde{Y}=k\mid\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{Q}_{kk}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\sum_{s=1}^{K}M_{ks}Q_{sk}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\left(M_{kk}Q_{kk}+\sum_{s\neq k}M_{ks}Q_{sk}\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\left(\frac{(1-\epsilon)\rho_{k}}{(1-\epsilon)\rho_{k}+\epsilon/K}Q_{kk}+\sum_{s=1}^{K}\frac{\rho_{s}\cdot\epsilon/K}{(1-\epsilon)\rho_{k}+\epsilon/K}Q_{sk}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}Q_{kk}+\frac{\epsilon}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s=1}^{K}\rho_{s}Q_{sk}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=k\mid\hat{f}\right]+\frac{\epsilon}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=s\mid\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\psi+\frac{\epsilon}{K}.

∎

Proof of Equation (
A56
).

By the definition of
\hat{\zeta}_{k}
in (
20
),

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{k}

\displaystyle:=\max_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)-\min_{l\neq k}\left(\hat{V}^{\mathrm{upp}}_{kl}-V_{kl}\right)

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\left(\frac{\xi}{K}\cdot\frac{1+\nu(1+2\xi)}{1+\nu\xi}-\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}(1+2\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}})}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad-\left(\frac{\xi}{K}\cdot\frac{1-\nu}{1+\nu\xi}-\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{1-\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}\right)\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\xi}{K}\cdot\frac{2\nu(1+\xi)}{1+\nu\xi}-\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\left(\frac{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}(1+2\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}})}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}-\frac{1-\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\xi}{K}\cdot\frac{2\nu(1+\xi)}{1+\nu\xi}-\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{\left(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}+2\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}{\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}\right|.

Then, applying the triangle inequality gives us:

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{\xi}{K}\cdot\frac{2\nu(1+\xi)}{1+\nu\xi}-\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{2\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}})}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\left|\frac{2\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}})}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}-\frac{\left(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}+2\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}{\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\frac{\xi}{K}\cdot\frac{2\nu(1+\xi)}{1+\nu\xi}-\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{2\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}})}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}\right|+\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{\left(\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}-\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}{\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}.

Next, the monotonicity of the function
(\nu,\epsilon)\mapsto(\nu(1+\epsilon))/((1+\nu\epsilon))
in both inputs allows us to conclude that:

\displaystyle\hat{\zeta}_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{upp}}}{K}\cdot\frac{2\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{upp}})}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{upp}}}-\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{2\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}})}{1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{\left(\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}-\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}{\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2}{K}\cdot\frac{\left(\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}-\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)+\left[\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\left(\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right)^{2}-\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\left(\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)^{2}\right]+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{upp}}\left(\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{upp}}-\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}{\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{upp}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}}{K}\cdot\frac{\left(\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}-\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}{\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{low}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)\left(1+\hat{\nu}^{\mathrm{upp}}\hat{\xi}^{\mathrm{low}}\right)}.

∎

Proof of Equation (
A57
).

We begin by proving the first equation, namely

\displaystyle\tilde{\psi}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\psi+\frac{\epsilon}{K}+\frac{\epsilon\nu}{K}\left(2\phi-1\right).

It follows directly from the general estimating equation (
28
) that

\displaystyle\tilde{\psi}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,\tilde{Y}=k\mid\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{Q}_{kk}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s=1}^{K}M_{ks}Q_{sk}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(M_{kk}Q_{kk}+\sum_{s\neq k}M_{ks}Q_{sk}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left((1-\epsilon)Q_{kk}+\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\frac{\epsilon}{K}\left(1+\nu\right)Q_{sk}+\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}^{\mathrm{c}}_{k}}\frac{\epsilon}{K}\left(1-\nu\right)Q_{sk}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\psi+\frac{\epsilon(1+\nu)}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}Q_{sk}+\frac{\epsilon(1-\nu)}{K^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}^{\mathrm{c}}_{k}}Q_{sk}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\psi+\frac{\epsilon(1+\nu)}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=s\mid\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\epsilon(1-\nu)}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}^{\mathrm{c}}_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=s\mid\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\psi+\frac{\epsilon}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s=1}^{K}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=s\mid\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\epsilon\nu}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=s\mid\hat{f}\right]-\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}^{\mathrm{c}}_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=s\mid\hat{f}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\psi+\frac{\epsilon}{K}+\frac{\epsilon\nu}{K}\left(2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=k,Y=s\mid\hat{f}\right]-1\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\psi+\frac{\epsilon}{K}+\frac{\epsilon\nu}{K}\left(2\phi-1\right),

where

\displaystyle\phi

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=l,Y=k\mid\hat{f}\right].

This completes the first part of the proof.

Let us now prove the second equation, namely

\displaystyle\tilde{\phi}

\displaystyle=\phi-\epsilon(1-\nu)\left(\phi-\frac{1}{2}\right).

It follows directly from the general estimating equation (
28
) that

\displaystyle\tilde{\phi}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{f}(X)=l,\tilde{Y}=k\mid\hat{f}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\tilde{Q}_{kl}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\sum_{s=1}^{K}M_{ks}Q_{sl}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\left(M_{kk}Q_{kl}+\sum_{s\neq k}M_{ks}Q_{sl}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\left((1-\epsilon)Q_{kl}+\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\frac{\epsilon}{K}\left(1+\nu\right)Q_{sl}+\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}^{\mathrm{c}}_{k}}\frac{\epsilon}{K}\left(1-\nu\right)Q_{sk}\right)

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\frac{Q_{kl}}{K}+\frac{\epsilon\left(1+\nu\right)}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\frac{Q_{sl}}{K}+\frac{\epsilon\left(1-\nu\right)}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{l\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}^{\mathrm{c}}_{k}}\frac{Q_{sl}}{K}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\phi+\frac{\epsilon\left(1+\nu\right)}{K}\frac{K}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}_{k}}\frac{Q_{sk}}{K}+\frac{\epsilon\left(1-\nu\right)}{K}\frac{K}{2}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{B}^{\mathrm{c}}_{k}}\frac{Q_{sk}}{K}

\displaystyle=(1-\epsilon)\phi+\frac{\epsilon\left(1+\nu\right)}{2}\phi+\frac{\epsilon\left(1-\nu\right)}{2}(1-\phi).

This concludes the second part of the proof.

∎

A7.5
Extensions of preliminary theoretical results

Proof of Theorem
A1
.

The proof strategy is similar to that of Theorem
1
.
By definition of the conformity score function in (
1
),
Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})
if and only if
\hat{s}(X_{n+1},Y_{n+1})\leq\hat{\tau}_{Y_{n+1}}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\right]+\left(\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\right]\right).

The proof is then completed by noting that the second term on the right-hand-side above can be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\rho_{k}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid Y_{n+1}=k\right]-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\in\hat{C}(X_{n+1})\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\rho_{k}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq\hat{\tau}\mid Y_{n+1}=k\right]-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq\hat{\tau}\mid\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\rho_{k}F_{k}^{k}(\hat{\tau})-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(\hat{\tau})\right]\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta(\hat{\tau})\right].

∎

Proof of Corollary
3
.

By Theorem
A1
, it suffices to prove
\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta(t)\right]\geq 0
for all
t\in\mathbb{R}
.
To establish that, note that combining (
A68
) with Proposition
1
gives:

\displaystyle\Delta(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\rho_{k}F_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right]=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\rho_{k}F_{k}^{k}(t)-\sum_{l=1}^{K}M_{kl}\tilde{\rho}_{k}F_{l}^{k}(t)\right].

To simplify the notation in the following, define

\displaystyle W_{kl}

\displaystyle:=\mathbb{P}[\tilde{Y}=k\mid X,Y=l]=M_{kl}\frac{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\rho_{l}}.

Then,

\displaystyle\Delta(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}F_{l}^{l}(t)-\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}\sum_{k=1}^{K}W_{kl}F_{l}^{k}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}F_{l}^{l}(t)-\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}\left[W_{ll}F_{l}^{l}(t)+\sum_{k\neq l}W_{kl}F_{l}^{k}(t)\right]

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}F_{l}^{l}(t)-\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}\left[W_{ll}F_{l}^{l}(t)+\left(\sum_{k\neq l}W_{kl}\right)\max_{k\neq l}F_{l}^{k}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}F_{l}^{l}(t)-\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}\left[W_{ll}F_{l}^{l}(t)+\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}W_{kl}\right)\max_{k\neq l}F_{l}^{k}(t)-W_{ll}\max_{k\neq l}F_{l}^{k}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}F_{l}^{l}(t)-\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}\left[W_{ll}F_{l}^{l}(t)+\max_{k\neq l}F_{l}^{k}(t)-W_{ll}\max_{k\neq l}F_{l}^{k}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\rho_{l}(1-W_{ll})\left[F_{l}^{l}(t)-\max_{k\neq l}F_{l}^{k}(t)\right]\geq 0,

where the last inequality is given by (
A71
).
This implies that
\Delta({\widehat{\tau}})\geq 0
almost-surely.
∎

A7.6
Prediction sets with marginal coverage

Proof of Theorem
A2
.

The proof strategy is similar to those of Theorem
A1
and Theorem
2
.
By definition of the conformity score function in (
1
), the event
Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})
occurs if and only if
\hat{s}(X_{n+1},Y_{n+1})\leq\hat{\tau}^{\mathrm{marg}}
.
Further, note that

\displaystyle\tilde{F}^{k}(t)=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t).

Therefore, the probability of a miscoverage event conditional on the data in
\mathcal{D}
can be decomposed as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\notin\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\notin\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\notin\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]-\mathbb{P}\left[\tilde{Y}_{n+1}\notin\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\left[1-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)>S^{k}_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})}\mid\mathcal{D},Y_{n+1}=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)>S^{k}_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})}\mid\mathcal{D},\tilde{Y}_{n+1}=k\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\left[1-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left[\rho_{k}-\rho_{k}F^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{\rho}_{k}+\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\hat{\Delta}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]+\left[\hat{\Delta}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\left\{1-\frac{\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}-\hat{\Delta}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right\}-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]+\left[\hat{\Delta}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sup_{i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}}\left\{1-\frac{i}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}-\hat{\Delta}(S_{(i)})+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right\}-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left[\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)\right].

By definition of
\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}
, for all
i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}
,

\displaystyle 1-\frac{i}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}-\hat{\Delta}(S_{(i)})+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\leq\alpha,

which implies a.s.

\displaystyle\sup_{i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}}\left\{1-\frac{i}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}-\hat{\Delta}(S_{(i)})+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right\}\leq\alpha.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha+\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left[\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)\right]\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*}).

The first expected value on the right-hand-side above can be bound using the DKW inequality, as made precise by Lemma
A5
.
This leads to:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\alpha+\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\min\left\{K^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}.

Finally, the remaining expected value can be bound as the corresponding term in the proof of Theorem
2
.
Let
U_{1},\ldots,U_{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}
be i.i.d. uniform random variables on
[0,1]
, and denote their order statistics as
U_{(1)},\ldots,U_{(n_{\mathrm{cal}})}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\overset{d}{=}\frac{\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}-U_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})}.

Therefore, it follows from (
17
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{cal}}]}\left\{\frac{i}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}-U_{(i)}\right\}\right]=c(n_{\mathrm{cal}}).

(A91)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\alpha+c(n_{\mathrm{cal}})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\min\left\{K^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=\alpha.

∎

Lemma A5
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
A2
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\min\left\{K^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}.

Proof of Lemma
A5
.

Note that, for any
k\in[K]
and
t\in\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\right)\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(V_{kk}-1\right)\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\right)\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left\{\left[\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)\right]-\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\right)\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right],

where in the last equality we used the fact that
V
has row sums equal to 1 because it is the inverse of
M
, which has row sums equal to 1.
Therefore,

\displaystyle|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right)\left(\max_{l\neq k}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\left(\max_{k\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)|\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right)\left(\max_{k,l\in[K]\,:\,l\neq k}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\max_{k\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)|\right)\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|

\displaystyle=2\left(\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right)\left(\max_{k,l\in[K]\,:\,l\neq k}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\left(\max_{k\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)|\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\left(\max_{k,l\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right),

and thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{k,l\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right].

As in the proof of Lemma
A1
, define

\displaystyle l_{\min}:=\arg\min_{l\in[K]}n_{j}.

\displaystyle n_{*}:=\min_{l\in[K]}n_{l}.

Then, combining the DKW inequality with a union bound leads to:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{k,l\in[K]}\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|\right]

\displaystyle\leq K^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2n_{*}}}.

Similarly, the DKW inequality also implies that, for any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|>\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]\leq\eta.

see Lemma
A6
.
Therefore, setting
\eta=1/n_{*}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|\right]\leq\left[2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right]\left[\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2n_{*}}}+\frac{1}{n_{*}}\right].

This concludes the proof that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\min\left\{K^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}.

∎

Lemma A6
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
A2
, for any
\eta>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|>\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]\leq\eta.

Proof of Lemma
A6
.

It follows from the definitions of
\hat{\Delta}(t)
and
\Delta(t)
, and from the DKW inequality, that, for any
\eta>0
and
k_{0}\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|>\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{k,l\in[K]}|\hat{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(t)|>\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq K^{2}\cdot\mathbb{P}\left[|\hat{F}_{l_{\min}}^{k_{0}}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k_{0}}_{l_{\min}}(t)|>\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2K^{2}\cdot\exp\left[-2n_{*}\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(1/\eta)}{2n_{*}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=2K^{2}\cdot\exp\left[-\log(2K^{2})-\log(1/\eta)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\eta.

∎

Proof of Theorem
A3
.

The proof combines the strategies from the proof of Theorem
3
and the proof of Theorem
A2
.
Define the events
\mathcal{A}_{1}=\{\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}=\emptyset\}
and
\mathcal{A}_{2}=\{\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}=1\}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]&\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\right]\\
&\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\right].\end{split}

(A92)

We will now separately bound the three terms on the right-hand-side of (
A92
).
The following notation will be useful for this purpose.
For all
i\in[n_{\mathrm{cal}}]
, let
U_{i}\sim\text{Uniform}(0,1)
be independent and identically distributed uniform random variables, and
denote their order statistics as
U_{(1)}<U_{(2)}<\ldots<U_{(n_{\mathrm{cal}})}
.

•

The probability of the event
\mathcal{A}_{1}
can be bound from above as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}=\emptyset\right]\leq\frac{1}{n_{*}}.

(A93)

To simplify the notation in the proof of (
A93
), define

\displaystyle d^{\mathrm{marg}}

\displaystyle\quad:=c(n_{\mathrm{cal}})+\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\frac{1+2\sqrt{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}.

Then, under Assumption
6
,

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(S_{(i)})+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(S_{(i)})-{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(i)})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d^{\mathrm{marg}}+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\sup_{t\in[0,1]}|\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}(t)|.

Further, it follows from Lemma
A6
and (
A76
) that, with probability at least
1-1/n_{*}
,

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d^{\mathrm{marg}}+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d^{\mathrm{marg}}+c(n_{\mathrm{cal}})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left(2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|\right)\frac{1+2\sqrt{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad=1.

This implies that the set
\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}
defined in (
A73
) is non-empty with probability at least
1-1/n_{*}
, because
n_{\mathrm{cal}}\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}
if
1-[\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})]\leq 1
.

•

Under
\mathcal{A}_{2}
, the second term on the right-hand-side of (
A92
) can be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},Y_{n+1})\leq S_{(1)}\mid\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle=F(S_{(1)})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}F^{k}_{k}(S_{(1)})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\sum_{l=1}^{K}V_{kl}\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(1)}).

Then, using Assumption
3
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right)\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(1)})

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\left(V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right)\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(1)})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\right)\right]\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(1)})

\displaystyle\qquad=\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\right)\right]\tilde{F}(S_{(1)}).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\right]&\leq\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(1)})\right]\\
&=\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[U_{(1)}\right]\\
&=\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\right)\right]\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}.\end{split}

(A94)

•

Under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
, by definition of
\hat{\mathcal{I}}^{\mathrm{marg}}
, for any
i\leq\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1
,

\displaystyle\frac{i}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right).

Therefore, choosing
i=\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1
, we get:

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right)+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}.

As in the proofs of Theorems
3
and
A2
, the probability of coverage conditional on the labeled data in
\mathcal{D}
can be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})+\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})

\displaystyle\qquad=\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})+\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})+\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}+\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})+\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad<1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right)+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})+\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]+\left[\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right].

The last term above can be bound by proceeding as in the proof of Theorem
3
:

\displaystyle\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-{\widehat{\Delta}}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}(t)|+(\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})),

where the expected value of the first term above can be bounded using Lemma
A5
, and the second term is given by

\displaystyle\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}\right)\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S^{k}_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\cdot\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left(\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\max_{l\neq k}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\left(\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\right)\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\max_{l\neq k}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left(\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\right)\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{\mathrm{cal}}}\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(i)})-\tilde{F}(S_{(i-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\max_{l\neq k}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right],

where the first inequality follows from the fact that, by definition of the order statistics,
\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\geq\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})
for all
l\in[K]
.
By a standard result on maximum uniform spacing,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{\mathrm{cal}}}\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(i)})-\tilde{F}(S_{(i-1)})\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}D_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\frac{1}{j},

where
D_{1}=U_{(1)}
,
D_{i}=U_{(i)}-U_{(i-1)}
for
i=2,\dots,n_{\mathrm{cal}}
, and
D_{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}=1-U_{(n_{\mathrm{cal}})}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left(\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\right)\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\frac{1}{j}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{l\neq k}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]\right].

By Assumption
2
, for any
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{l\neq k}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{l}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[f_{\max}\cdot\left[S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})}-S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\rho_{k}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\cdot\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\left(\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\sum_{k=1}^{K}\tilde{\rho}_{k}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{\mathrm{cal}}}|\tilde{F}(S_{(i)})-\tilde{F}(S_{(i-1)})|\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{\mathrm{cal}}}D_{i}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\frac{1}{j}.

Therefore, under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}-1)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left(\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\right)\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\frac{1}{j}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\frac{1}{j}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}1/j}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\cdot\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}+\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right].

At this point, we have proved that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\right]\\
&\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}+\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{i\in[n_{\mathrm{cal}}]}|\tilde{F}(S_{(i)})-\hat{F}(S_{(i})\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\cdot\min\left\{K^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}1/j}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\cdot\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}+\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\\
&\qquad=1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}+c(n_{\mathrm{cal}})\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{2\max_{k\in[K]}\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\rho_{k}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}|}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}\\
&\qquad\qquad\cdot\min\left\{K^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K^{2})+\log(n_{*})}{2}}\right\}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}1/j}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\cdot\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}+\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right]\\
&\qquad=1-\alpha+2\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}+\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}1/j}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}\cdot\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}+\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right],\end{split}

(A95)

where the inequality above follows from Lemma
A5
and the bound in (
A82
).

Finally, combining (
A92
) with (
A93
), (
A94
), and (
A95
) leads to the desired result:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-\alpha+2\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})+\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}+\frac{1}{n_{*}}+\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\right)\right]\frac{1}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sum_{j=1}^{n_{\mathrm{cal}}+1}1/j}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}\cdot\left[\max_{k\in[K]}\frac{\rho_{k}V_{kk}-\tilde{\rho}_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}+\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\max_{k\in[K]}\left(\frac{\rho_{k}}{\tilde{\rho}_{k}}\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\right)\right].

∎

Proof of Theorem
A3
.

This proof combines elements of the proofs of Proposition
2
and Theorem
A2
.
Proceeding exactly as in the proof of Theorem
A2
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\notin\hat{C}^{\mathrm{marg}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*}),0\right\}-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\{\hat{\Delta}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*}),0\}-\left[\Delta(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}}}{n_{\mathrm{cal}}}-\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*}),0\right\}\right]-\delta^{\mathrm{marg}}(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\hat{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})-\tilde{F}(S_{(\hat{i}^{\mathrm{marg}})})\right]+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|,

using the fact that
\inf_{t\in{\rm I}\kern-1.47495pt{\rm R}}\Delta(t)\geq\delta(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*})
implies

|\max\{{\widehat{\Delta}}(t)-\delta(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*}),0\}-(\Delta(t)-\delta(n_{\mathrm{cal}},n_{*}))|\leq|\hat{\Delta}(t)-\Delta(t)|

for all
t\in\mathbb{R}
.
The proof is then completed by proceeding as in the proof of Theorem
A2
.
∎

A7.7
Prediction sets with calibration-conditional coverage

Proof of Theorem
A4
.

The proof is similar to that of Theorem
2
.
Suppose
Y_{n+1}=k
, for some
k\in[K]
.
We assume without loss of generality that
\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|\neq 0
; otherwise, there is no label contamination and the result is trivially true.
By definition of the conformity score function in (
1
), the event
k\notin\hat{C}(X_{n+1})
occurs if and only if
\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)>\hat{\tau}_{k}
.
We will assume without loss of generality that
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}\neq\emptyset
and
\hat{i}_{k}=\min\{i\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}\}
; otherwise,
\hat{\tau}_{k}=1
and the result trivially holds.
As in the proof of Theorem
2
, the probability of miscoverage conditional on
Y_{n+1}=k
and on the labeled data in
\mathcal{D}
can be bounded from above as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}[\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)]+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left[\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right].

We know from Lemma
A2
that, for any
\gamma_{1}>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|>2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\gamma_{1})}{2n_{*}}}\right]\leq\gamma_{1}.

Similarly, it follows directly from the DKW inequality that, for any
\gamma_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]>\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma_{2})}{2n_{k}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\gamma_{2}.

Therefore, for any
\gamma,\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}>0
such that
\gamma=\gamma_{1}+\gamma_{2}
, with probability at least
1-\gamma
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\gamma_{1})}{2n_{*}}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma_{2})}{2n_{k}}}.

Finally, setting

\displaystyle\gamma_{1}=\frac{\gamma}{2}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|},

\displaystyle\gamma_{2}=\gamma\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|}\right),

gives the desired result; that is, with probability at least
1-\gamma
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\leq\alpha.

∎

Proof of Theorem
A5
.

The proof follows an approach similar to that of the proof of Theorem
3
.
Define the events
\mathcal{A}_{1}=\{\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}=\emptyset\}
and
\mathcal{A}_{2}=\{\hat{i}_{k}=1\}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D},Y=k\right]\\
&\qquad\leq\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}\right]+\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\\
&\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right].\end{split}

(A96)

We will now separately bound the three terms on the right-hand-side of (
A96
).
The following notation will be useful for this purpose.
For all
i\in[n_{k}]
, let
U_{i}\sim\text{Uniform}(0,1)
be independent and identically distributed uniform random variables, and
denote their order statistics as
U_{(1)}<U_{(2)}<\ldots<U_{(n_{k})}
.

•

The probability of the event
\mathcal{A}_{1}
can be bound from above as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}=\emptyset\right]\leq\gamma_{2}.

(A97)

To simplify the notation in the proof of (
A97
), define

\displaystyle d_{k}:=\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma_{1})}{2n_{k}}}+4\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\gamma_{2})}{2n_{*}}}.

Then, under Assumption
7
,

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=1-\alpha-\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})+\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+\Delta_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d_{k}+\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+\sup_{t\in[0,1]}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|.

Further, it follows from Lemma
A2
and (
A80
) that, with probability at least
1-\gamma_{2}
,

\displaystyle 1-\left(\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d_{k}+\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\gamma_{2})}{2n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 1-d_{k}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma_{1})}{2n_{k}}}+4\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\gamma_{2})}{2n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad=1.

This implies that the set
\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
defined in (
15
) is non-empty with probability at least
1-\gamma_{2}
, because
n_{k}\in\hat{\mathcal{I}}_{k}
if
1-[\alpha+{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)]\leq 1
.

•

Under
\mathcal{A}_{2}
, the second term on the right-hand-side of (
A96
) can be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left[\hat{s}(X_{n+1},k)\leq S^{k}_{(1)}\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle=F^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)}).

Therefore, using Assumption
3
, we can write that, for any
a>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\geq a\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[F^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\geq a\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\left(V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\right)\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(1)})\geq a\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[U_{(1)}\geq\frac{a}{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\left(1-\frac{a}{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}\right)^{n_{k}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n_{k}}\cdot\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{a}.

In particular, choosing
a=(V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|)/(n_{k}\bar{\gamma})
, for any
\bar{\gamma}\in(0,1)
, gives us:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{2}\right]\leq\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}\bar{\gamma}}\right]\geq 1-\bar{\gamma}.

(A98)

•

By proceeding as in the proof of Theorem
3
, using Assumption
2
we obtain that, under
\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}\left[Y\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\\
&\qquad<1-\alpha+\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left[\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)-\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\Delta_{k}(t)-{\widehat{\Delta}}_{k}(t)|\\
&\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|.\end{split}

(A99)

Now, recall that for any
\bar{\gamma}\in(0,1)
, by the DKW inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right]>\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\bar{\gamma})}{2n_{k}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\gamma}.

Similarly, we know from Lemma
A2
that, for any
\bar{\gamma}\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|>2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(1/\bar{\gamma})}{2n_{*}}}\right]\leq\bar{\gamma}.

It remains to bound the last term on the right-hand-side of (
A99
).
Note that, for any
\bar{\gamma}\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{2\leq i\leq n_{k}}\left|\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i)})-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(S^{k}_{(i-1)})\right|>\bar{\gamma}\right]=\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i}>\bar{\gamma}\right],

where
U_{i}\sim\text{Uniform}(0,1)
are independent and identically distributed uniform random variables for all
i\in[n_{k}]
, and
D_{1}=U_{(1)}
,
D_{i}=U_{(i)}-U_{(i-1)}
for
i=2,\dots,n_{k}
, and
D_{n_{k}+1}=1-U_{(n_{k})}
.

Further, by combining the Markov inequality with a standard result on the asymptotic behavior of the maximum uniform spacing, we obtain that, for any
a>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i}>a\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i}\right]=\frac{1}{a}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}.

Therefore, setting
a=(\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}1/j)/(n_{k}\bar{\gamma})
for any
\bar{\gamma}>0
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\max_{1\leq i\leq n_{k}+1}D_{i}>\frac{1}{\bar{\gamma}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right]\leq\bar{\gamma}.

With a union bound, this implies that, for any
\bar{\gamma}\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y\in C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]\mathbb{I}\left[\mathcal{A}_{1}^{c}\cap\mathcal{A}_{2}^{c}\right]>1-\alpha+\omega(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma,\bar{\gamma})\right]\leq\bar{\gamma},

(A100)

where

\displaystyle\omega(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma,\bar{\gamma})

\displaystyle\qquad=\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+\frac{1}{n_{k}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(3/\bar{\gamma})}{2n_{k}}}+2\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\sqrt{\frac{\log(2K)+\log(3/\bar{\gamma})}{2n_{*}}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\cdot\frac{f_{\max}}{f_{\min}}\cdot\frac{1}{n_{k}}\cdot\left[\log(n_{k}+1)+\frac{3}{\bar{\gamma}}\sum_{j=1}^{n_{k}+1}\frac{1}{j}\right].

Finally, combining (
A96
) with (
A97
), (
A98
)
, and (
A100
) leads to

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathbb{P}\left[Y_{n+1}\in\hat{C}^{\mathrm{cc}}(X_{n+1})\mid\mathcal{D},Y=k\right]\geq 1-\alpha+\omega(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma,\bar{\gamma})+\frac{V_{kk}+\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{n_{k}\bar{\gamma}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\gamma_{2}+2\bar{\gamma}.

Thus, the desired result is obtained by setting

\displaystyle\bar{\gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{\gamma-\gamma_{2}}{2}=\frac{\gamma}{2}\left(1-\frac{1}{2}\cdot\frac{\sum_{\l\neq k}|V_{kl}|}{\sum_{l=1}^{K}|V_{kl}|}\right).

∎

Proof of Theorem
A4
.

This proof combines elements of the proofs of Proposition
2
and Theorem
A4
.
Suppose
Y_{n+1}=k
, for some
k\in[K]
.
Proceeding exactly as in the proof of Theorem
A4
, we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[Y\notin C(X,S^{k}_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\mid Y=k,\mathcal{D}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}\right\}\right]-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}\right\}-\left[\Delta_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\left[1-\frac{\hat{i}_{k}}{n_{k}}-\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*}),-\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}\right\}\right]-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left[\hat{F}_{k}^{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*}),0\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\left[\Delta_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\alpha-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*},\gamma)+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|+\sup_{t\in\mathbb{R}}\left[\hat{F}^{k}_{k}(t)-\tilde{F}^{k}_{k}(t)\right].

using the fact that
\inf_{t\in{\rm I}\kern-1.47495pt{\rm R}}\Delta_{k}(t)\geq\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*})-\sqrt{\log(1/\gamma)/(2n_{k})}
implies

\displaystyle\left|\max\left\{\hat{\Delta}_{k}(S_{(\hat{i}_{k})})+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*}),0\right\}-\left[\Delta_{k}(t)+\sqrt{\frac{\log(1/\gamma)}{2n_{k}}}-\delta^{\mathrm{cc}}(n_{k},n_{*})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq|\hat{\Delta}_{k}(t)-\Delta_{k}(t)|

for all
t\in\mathbb{R}
.
∎

Comparison to worst-case coverage bounds

Proof of Corollary
2
.

The expression for
\Delta_{k}(t)
in (
12
) can be equivalently rewritten as

\displaystyle\Delta_{k}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l\neq k}V_{kl}\left(\tilde{F}_{l}^{k}(t)-\tilde{F}_{k}^{k}(t)\right),

which implies

\displaystyle-\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|\leq\Delta_{k}(t)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l\neq k}|V_{kl}|,\qquad\forall t\in\mathbb{R}.

The proof is then completed by applying Theorem
1
.
∎

A8.4
Robustness to model mis-specification

This section demonstrates the robustness of our adaptive methods to other types of mis-specifications in the label contamination process, going beyond the issue of estimating the noise parameter
\epsilon
of the randomized response model described in Section
A3.1
.
For this purpose, we generate synthetic data with
K=4
possible labels as explained in Section
4.1
, but using a label contamination process with a block-like structure.
That is, the contamination process is described by a transition matrix
T\in[0,1]^{K\times K}
, defined as
T_{kl}=\mathbb{P}[\tilde{Y}=k\mid X,Y=l]
for all
l,k\in[K]
, given by
T=(1-\epsilon)I_{K}+\epsilon/K\cdot B_{K,2}
, where
B_{K,2}
is a block-diagonal matrix with
K/2
constant blocks equal to
J_{2}
—the
2\times 2
matrix of ones.
Then, we apply the optimistic version of Algorithm
1
under the mis-specified assumption that
T=(1-\epsilon)I_{K}+\epsilon/K\cdot J_{K}
, separately using either the correct value of
\epsilon
or a plug-in empirical estimate obtained from an independent model-fitting data set as in Section
A3.1.4
.
Consistently with the previous section, we refer to the former method as
Adaptive+
and to the latter as
Adaptive+ (plug-in)
.
Figure
A41
compares the performances of our
Adaptive+
and
Adaptive+ (plug-in)
to that of the standard conformal inference approach, as a function of the true
\epsilon
and of the number of clean model-fitting data points used to estimate this parameter.
The number of calibration data points here is set equal to 10,000.
The results show that both methods are quite robust to model mis-specification and can generally produce more informative prediction sets compared to the standard benchmark.

Figure A41
:
Performances of different conformal methods on simulated data, as a function of the number of clean samples used to estimate a mis-specified contamination model.
The true model matrix
M
has a block-diagonal structure, and the results are stratified based on the contamination strength.
Other details are as in Figure
2
.

Finally, Figure
A42
demonstrates that our methods also enjoy similar robustness under different label contamination processes, focusing specifically on a transition matrix
T=(1-\epsilon)I_{K}+\epsilon/K\cdot U_{K}
, where
U_{K}
is a matrix of i.i.d. uniform random numbers on
[0,1]
, standardized to have its columns sum to one.

Figure A42
:
Performances of different conformal methods on simulated data, as a function of the number of clean samples used to estimate a mis-specified contamination model.
The contamination model matrix
M
has a random structure.
Other details are as in Figure
A41
.

Appendix A9
Additional details on numerical experiments

A9.1
Experiments with heteroscedastic decision-tree model

We set
d=50
and generate each sample of features
X\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
independently as follows:

X_{1}=+1
w.p.
3/4
, and
X_{1}=-1
w.p.
1/4
;

X_{2}=+1
w.p.
3/4
, and
X_{2}=-2
w.p.
1/4
;

X_{3}=+1
w.p.
1/4
, and
X_{3}=-2
w.p.
1/2
;

X_{4}
is uniformly distributed on
\{1,\ldots,4\}
;
and
X_{j}\overset{i.i.d.}{\sim}\mathcal{N}(0,1)
for all
j\in\{5,\ldots,d\}
.
The labels
Y
belong to one of
K=4
possible classes, and their conditional distribution given
X=x
is given by the decision tree shown in Figure
A45
, which only depends on the first four features.

Figure A45
:
A toy heteroscedastic decision-tree model for
P_{Y\mid X}
used to generate syntheticin some numerical experiments.
